([po4yka/rust-skills](https://github.com/po4yka/rust-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rust-tdd) · ★7 · 4 copies)
- **rust-tdd**: Use when implementing Rust features test-first, reproducing Rust bugs with regression tests, characterizing legacy Rust, or applying red-green-refactor with cargo test. Covers boundary and equivalence cases, proptest shrinking, mockall expectations, rstest fixtures, async/concurrent/CLI tests, and coverage interpretation. Not a substitute for system-wide test strategy or benchmarking. ([Lu1sDV/skillsmd](https://github.com/Lu1sDV/skillsmd/tree/HEAD/rust-tdd) · ★2)
- **rust-tdd**: Write Rust test-first on this CAO repository. Use as soon as a feature is added, a bug is fixed, or the solver, the constraints, the geometry or the booleans are touched — and whenever one wonders where to put a test, how to compare f64, or how to test code with no net. ([larissa-3d/project_cad](https://github.com/larissa-3d/project_cad/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rust-tdd) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **rust-tdd**: Appliquer le TDD dans le workspace Rust Palet Vendéen (backend Actix, shared, frontend/mobile Dioxus 0.7) — cycle red-green-refactor, organisation des tests, outils et commandes de test, exemples concrets. ([b-laffitte-dev/palet](https://github.com/b-laffitte-dev/palet/tree/HEAD/.vibe/skills/rust-tdd))
- **rust-tdd**: Test-driven development enforcement for Rust. Requires failing tests before implementation. Use when implementing features, fixing bugs, or when code quality discipline is needed. ([hwatkins/my-skills](https://github.com/hwatkins/my-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rust-tdd))
- **rust-tdd**: Cargo TDD with approval-aware execution: failing test for the right reason, authorized implementation, green, refactor, fmt/clippy. Trigger: tdd, red-green-refactor, test first, cargo test. ([igmarin/agent-profiles](https://github.com/igmarin/agent-profiles/tree/HEAD/plugins/rust-developer/skills/rust-tdd))
